TD & RA1 Reference Map

Prepared by Codex · 2026-09-11 04:46 WIB · local, uncommitted review

Explicit local firepower and mixed-load cargo review

163 actors, 282 assigned source links. This adds 15 pilot heroes to the earlier 148 ordinary actors. The scope is TD GDI, TD Nod, RA1 Allies and RA1 Soviets, not an all-faction census.

Across all displayed rows, the current normalization produces 233 range source votes and 222 weapon-rate source votes; 105 actors have a rate estimate. Identity links include unarmed counterparts, excluded upgrades and sparse hero populations, so 282 is not the expected denominator for every combat axis. These counts are not a project completion percentage.

Among the 55 armed ordinary actors with at least three assigned sources, 54/55 have all-source range votes and 54/55 have all-source nominal weapon-rate votes. These coverage figures exclude the separate hero lane. Spy’s CA and DTA counterparts are unarmed: their range/DPS are N/A, not missing gun values.

The reference profiles contain 195 peer weapon-cycle records, 302 independent peer range records, 477 DTA range records and 444 DTA weapon-timer records. These are corpus records, including variants; they are not counts of independent votes.

How to read the numbers: assigned refs count identity matches. Each estimate displays its usable-source count. Multiple eligible variants share one source vote. Hover for exclusion reasons and expand Weapon calculation details for source values, timing and scope. Thirty-three reviewed CA upgraded replacements remain visible but do not enter base estimates.

Rates are damage per source tick, not damage per second. Peers use the selected base weapon; Cameo uses its selected baseline armament aggregate. Rates exclude armor response and movement/rearming/animation gates unless explicitly modeled. They are nominal comparison metrics, not sustained battle DPS. DTA timing uses an inspected OpenTS model; applicability to DTA’s actual runtime remains unverified. Flame-pulse rates use the full-damage centerline region. Special cases remain open rather than receiving fabricated universal values.

The permanent ordinary Cameo baseline has 893 rows; the separately recovered hero baseline has 83 rows from hash-matched original inputs. Both self-votes and normalization populations are frozen. Current columns still show the current local ledger. No new HP/cost balance writeback, source publication or game launch is included.

Current test configuration: Shared-default unconditional multipliers remain removed without compensation. Under Aedis's corrected local-factor rule, 30 pilot actors / 100 weapon contexts now have their saved unit-local firepower factors baked into explicit damage. Five isolated weapon variants protect other users. Main damage uses the approved 10-point grid; Monster Tank's 126262.5 rounds to 126260. Percentage strength is retained with explicit precision metadata where needed. Conditional rank/upgrade/status mechanics remain. Incoming DamageMultiplier compensation is still unresolved; this is not a fully retired all-roster multiplier system.

Reviewed weapon models: Tomahawk follows the launched missile to its landed impact; Laser Turret and Venom use one laser impact per shot; Pitbull uses the ground rocket burst; CRAM shows its cold sequence and post-spin-up rate. XO and Kamov use their selected primary weapon, with secondary armaments kept separate. Aircraft rates while ammunition remains are not sustained sortie rates.

Commando: both GDI and Nod now share RMBO identities from CA, DTA and OpenRA TD. Three identity references currently yield two normalized votes: the OpenRA TD hero population has only one usable actor, below the minimum three needed for normalization. Its raw source values remain visible.

Reference comparison: CA Flame Tower and Stealth Tank use the approved common authored damage basis for their split channels; CA sniper uses a named healthy target and an uncapped damage value. These add three peer profiles, for 195 total. The ordinary three-source cohort now has 54/55 full nominal-rate coverage, with Spy's unarmed CA/DTA counterparts accepted N/A. Still open: combined per-armor source curves, roster-wide Versus proposals, sparse hero normalization, class calibration, shrapnel battlefield scenarios and playtesting. Numeric coverage is not playtest readiness.

Targeting corrections: Recon Bike and EMP Grenadier now expose their full applicable damage channels to Air. Soviet Tesla chains preserve each firing parent role; 36 reviewed routes, including isolated firepower variants, have matching parent, FireShrapnel emitter and fragment target masks, including custom exclusions. This is static coverage of the reviewed routes, not whole-game certification. V2 Tesla SCUD still inherits a ground-only parent; whether to give the parent Air targeting is a pending design clarification.

Calibration and cargo checks: Current unsigned-anchor diagnostics flag 37 actors across 23 classes. The checker now values authored mixed InitialUnits loads: 11 valid full loads, 8 price mismatches, and 3 unresolved loads. Nod Chinook's eight listed passengers occupy weight 11 against capacity 8; GDI Landing Craft and Nod Transport Submarine have no initial load. Armed cargo uses K 1.25 inside the combat formula, not a purchase surcharge. No prices or loadouts were changed. Tier suitability and runtime loading remain separate checks.

Model yardstick: dedicated fragment copies change the existing live-weapon median range to 5,938. All derived sidecars were regenerated together; the model metadata records this yardstick. Authored HP/cost values and frozen comparison baselines are preserved; current weapon damage reflects the local-factor conversion. These model estimates do not authorize price writeback.

163 of 163 actors shown

Current four-faction reference review. Estimates are unapproved comparison values, not applied balance targets. Hero-tagged rows use a separate hero-only normalization population and frozen Cameo self-vote.
